# Permanent places — accuracy audit of every live recurring place (2026-11-05)

**Trigger:** Örebro läns museum e-mailed that the app shows wrong information about them (building closed for renovation since 2018, reopens late 2027; the app said "Öppet tisdag till söndag"). The CEO asked how many other permanent places are wrong.

**Method:** all live recurring events (`isRecurring`, `/admin/events?isEnd=false`) were pulled with their venue data (1,365). 1) Automated scan of every website for closure phrases + HTTP status. 2) 56 verification agents (about 25–28 places each) checked every place against its official website / kommun page: status, opening hours and season vs the published recurrence, description claims, price (isFree). 3) Separate checks for duplicates and events attached to the wrong venue. Nothing was edited in the app during the audit.

## Headline numbers

| | Count |
|---|---|
| Live recurring places audited | 1365 |
| Verified against an official source | 1365 |
| Correct as published (keep) | 455 |
| Needs a correction (hours / season / description / price) | 797 |
| Should be unpublished (closed, moved, booking-only, unverifiable) | 113 |
| Not verified (agent output missing) | 0 |
| &nbsp;&nbsp;— wrong opening hours or season | 696 |
| &nbsp;&nbsp;— wrong description | 151 |
| &nbsp;&nbsp;— wrong isFree flag | 208 |
| Closed right now (renovation / permanently / moved) but shown as open | 27 |
| Seasonal places published as open all year (closed now) | 207 |
| Not a drop-in visitor place (booking-only, ticketed venue) | 51 |
| Duplicate recurring events of the same place | 14 events in 7 groups |
| Events attached to another place's venue (wrong address/pin) | 22 |

### By publishing batch

| Batch | Verified | Keep | Fix | Unpublish | Keep % |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Aug–Sep 2026 (template hours, no per-place check) | 1130 | 271 | 748 | 111 | 24% |
| 1 Oct 2026 (Jönköping region batch, before the per-place process) | 57 | 28 | 29 | 0 | 49% |
| 13 Oct–3 Nov 2026 (Gävleborg, Södermanland, Värmland, Halland — researched per place) | 178 | 156 | 20 | 2 | 88% |

Status distribution: OPEN 1028, SEASON_ONLY 207, NOT_A_VISITOR_PLACE 51, UNCLEAR 50, CLOSED_PERMANENT 13, CLOSED_RENOVATION 12, MOVED 4

Note: "wrong hours" is strict — it includes cases where the published window overruns the official closing time by 30–60 minutes, as well as places published daily that are closed several days a week.

## Root cause

- The Aug–Sep 2026 batch (~1,130 places) was created from template values: 504 events carry exactly `daily 10:00–16:00 to 2027-12-31`, 179 `daily 10:00–18:00`; 455 are marked free although many are paid venues. Opening status, season, hours and price were not checked against the official source per place.
- The 1 Oct 2026 batch (Jönköping region, 57) used real hours more often but still copied weekday windows onto weekends and missed seasonal closures.
- The 13 Oct–3 Nov batches (Gävleborg, Södermanland, Värmland+Halland) were researched per place from official sources; their remaining issues are small (a season end date one day off, isFree left false on free outdoor places, two booking-only farms that should not be drop-in places).
- Also found: website fields that now point to casino spam (Solna simhall, Tolvåkerbadet, Långe Erik, hantverkfalkangen.se, steningeslott.com) and dead domains; these links must be replaced.

## Remediation plan

1. Unpublish (reversible, venue `isPublished:false` or move to a `[Dold]` holding venue) every place in A and B.
2. Cut `endDate` / set the 2027 `startDate` for every seasonal place in C.
3. Re-set recurrence from the official hours for D, set `isFree` for E, rewrite descriptions for F.
4. Re-point G to their own venues (create the venue at the real address), rename H, unpublish the extra copies in I.
5. Manual check of J, then keep or unpublish.
6. New rule: no permanent place goes live without an official source for status + hours + price, and every live place is re-verified each quarter (`tools/permanent-places/` has the scan + verification brief).
